## Colombia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Colombia's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **27 distinct works: 26 student books and 1 teacher guide, all Colombia Ministerio de Educación Nacional (MEN), in Spanish** — Escuela Nueva Matemáticas cartillas Grades 1–5 (11), Nivelemos Matemáticas student workbooks Grades 1–4 (4), Aulas sin fronteras Grade 6 Unidad 1 student book and its teacher guide (2), Postprimaria student guides Grades 6–9 (4), Secundaria Activa student modules Grades 6–9 (4), and Vamos a Aprender Matemáticas 10 and 11 (2) — 3,928 PDF pages — and placed 293 teaching items: 288 from the student books and 5 teacher-evidence items from the one teacher guide (Book 23). SET CORRECTIONS carried exactly: (1) the expansion map's “28 student + 1 teacher = 29” over-counts by 2 — textbooks.tsv carries duplicate records of Escuela Nueva Grado 1 Cartilla 1 and Grado 2 Cartilla 1 (old blob/main rows for the same two works that also landed as release assets); the distinct set is 27 works and the duplicates are cited once (the library records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records); (2) the set in hand contains NO Grade 3 Cartilla 3 — Grade 3 holds Cartillas 1–2 plus the Nivelemos Grade 3 workbook, and Cartilla 2 completes Unidades 6–9; nothing in hand is a mislabelled Cartilla 3, and no never-published ruling is needed or made; (3) Nivelemos Matemáticas Grado 1 (Book 10) has NO asset in any GitHub release — it was read from the MEN source file itself, landed at exactly the census size (5,020,415 bytes) with EOF intact; the library side still owes that file a GitHub landing. The two Vamos a Aprender books (Books 26–27) are image-only viewer assemblies with no text layer, read from rendered pages (covers, printed contents pp. 6–7 and body spot-checks — Matemáticas 10 printed p. 110 “Teorema del seno”, Matemáticas 11 printed p. 120 “Derivada de una función en un punto”); printed page = PDF page in the sampled range. In the sections below the books are cited as Books 1–27 in the placements' order. Colombia COUNTS in a lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of its student-book rows; Book 23's rows are TEACHER EVIDENCE — the guide companions Book 15 with teaching plans, activity scripts and answers and teaches no student content itself — cited as the evidence kind it is and never the basis of the count (the standing Ghana evidence precedent for guide rows). Book errors: none material at placement grain — the Secundaria Activa Grade 9 module's contents page misprints two of its own headings (“Captítulo”, “Undiad 2”); the content is unaffected and rows use the corrected unit names. The placements' named exclusions — presentation letters, “how this book works” pages, author/imprint pages, transversal civics/health/environment themes, practice/evaluation apparatus (“Practica más”, “Evaluación del aprendizaje”, answer keys) and the Escuela Nueva “Estándares/Competencias” boxes at each unidad opening (MEN's own curriculum-standard statements; the taught content is placed at the guías themselves) — are stocked nowhere. The Círculos de Aprendizaje integrated books and the Retos para Gigantes Transición weekly guides are NOT in this set (filed as integrado, not matematicas) and are stocked nowhere. Colombia's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Maths book (27/27) has been read and placed, on the in-hand definition.

## Andorra added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Andorra's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **4 documents, all of them official Escola Andorrana curriculum programmes published by decree in the Butlletí Oficial del Principat d'Andorra (BOPA); 0 pupil (student) books** — and placed 679 curriculum-requirement items. A programme states what is REQUIRED to be taught; it teaches nothing itself. Every Andorra item in this section is therefore TEACHER EVIDENCE — a curriculum requirement, worded as a requirement — cited as the evidence kind it is; Andorra is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until Andorran pupil books are in hand and read. Set correction, as the placements prove it: the expansion map recorded 3 books in hand; the true set is 4 — the grades 1–6 programme (Decret 376/2021, Annex VII) is held in the GitHub repo but sits in no staging books.json, which is why the map missed it. All four programmes are Andorra's OWN (Govern d'Andorra, in Catalan); the Spanish and French systems also taught in Andorra (Spain's Anaya and Innovamat manuals; France's Le Livre Scolaire) are list-only names on official school book lists — no file is held, so they are cited nowhere in this course and credited to no country — and the Escola Andorrana itself has no commercial pupil-textbook series, so programmes are all that exist to hold. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct stated requirement — each specific competency, and each distinct entry in the programmes' learning-resource tables, tagged by cycle where the table splits by cycle — and a procedure entry that merely restates a concept entry is folded into it, not double-counted. The placements' named exclusions — the decree preambles, the introductions and competency rationale, the didactic orientations, the assessment criteria and end-of-cycle expectations, the synthesis tables (verified verbatim repeats of the competency sections), the attitudes/values lists, Book 1's glossary and opening summary table, and the Council of Europe cross-references — are stocked nowhere. Book 3's synthesis table words its C2 end-of-cycle expectation as modelling "situacions cientificotècniques" (scientific-technical) inside the social-sciences programme — a copy-over slip in the decree as published; Book 3's competency section itself is consistently social-sciences, and the slip is copied into no row below. Book 2 states its cone/polyhedron sections twice (forms table and models table, printed pp. 32–33) — counted once in the placements and stocked once. Printed page = PDF page in all four books (Books 2–4 carry the BOPA folio; Book 1's Portal PDF is paginated at the foot the same way). Into this lesson the placements stock 14 teacher-evidence items from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3.

**Book 1:** *Programa de matemàtiques de primera ensenyança* (curriculum programme, Grades 1–6) — Andorra, Govern d'Andorra, Escola Andorrana; Decret 376/2021, Annex VII; ISBN 978-99920-0-934-5; 688,455 bytes, 49 PDF pages; printed page = PDF page. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — an official curriculum programme: it states what is required to be taught and teaches nothing itself.

**Book 2:** *Programa de matemàtiques de segona ensenyança* (curriculum programme, Grades 7–10) — Andorra, Govern d'Andorra, Escola Andorrana; Decret 355/2023 (BOPA Núm. 91, 19-7-2023); ISBN 978-99920-0-847-2; 1,808,872 bytes, 56 PDF pages; printed page = PDF page (the BOPA folio). TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — an official curriculum programme: it states what is required to be taught and teaches nothing itself.

## Angola added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Angola's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **6 books, all pupil textbooks: Matemática, Manuais da 1.ª–6.ª Classe (Ministério da Educação — MED, Angola; updated editions of 2021, in Portuguese). There is no teacher guide in the Angola Maths set in hand, so every Angola row in this course is a pupil-textbook row — there are no teacher-evidence rows, and Angola counts in every lesson it stocks** — and placed 229 teaching items. The expansion map's “6 student + 0 teacher = 6” is correct: download-lists/angola.md, the phase3-staging checklist, textbooks.tsv, phase3-push-state.json (“pushed”) and the staging books.json files all give the same six distinct pupil textbooks, and nothing else. None of the six PDFs was on local disk (pusher-secured only), so all six were fetched fresh for the read from the books' recorded second official source, the ministry's Xilonga host, byte-exact against the records. One set nuance: the portal's 32-manual set contains no 2.ª Classe Matemática at all — the 2.ª book in hand (Book 2) exists only as an unlinked Xilonga file (MAT02-LR.pdf) found by pattern probe on 2026-10-23. A Guia Prático Matemática 6ª exists only as an exact-link reader copy and is NOT in hand — cited nowhere; if one is ever secured it enters as teacher evidence only. The Book 2 and Book 4 PDFs carry a massively duplicated text layer (the same text overprinted many times per page) — a production flaw of the files; the content is fully legible and both books were read in full from it. Twelve book errors are recorded in the placements; they are carried in the book entries below and taught corrected. The placements' named exclusions — the apresentação (ministerial foreword letter) and anthem/flag/civic front matter, the bibliografia pages, pure colouring, tracing and copying practice with no new concept, the editorial production notes printed inside the books, and exercise sets as such (exercises practise the item under which they sit) — are stocked nowhere. Printed-page bases: Books 1 and 3 — printed page = PDF page − 1; Books 2, 4, 5 and 6 — printed page = PDF page. Into this lesson the placements stock 8 items (all from pupil textbooks) from Book 3, Book 4, Book 5 and Book 6.

**Book 3:** *Matemática — Manual da 3.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 3) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Progresso Editora, 2021, 1.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-762-270-0; 1,798,728 bytes, 85 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page − 1). Book errors, taught corrected: printed p. 21 repeats the minuend misnomer “diminuendo ou aditivo” (teach: minuend); printed p. 36's decimal-multiplication worked example is internally inconsistent — it states 4,15 × 2,3, lays out 3,15 and prints 7,245 (correct: 4,15 × 2,3 = 9,545); the exercise under “Noção de circunferência” at printed p. 44 repeats the parallel-lines exercise verbatim (a copy-paste error in the book); printed p. 62 prints the false line “20 m = 200 cm” (correct: 20 m = 2 000 cm); printed p. 64's formula under decâmetro repeats “1 hm = 100 m” (correct: 1 dam = 10 m).

**Book 4:** *Matemática — Manual da 4.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 4) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Mayamba Editora, 2021, 1.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-761-296-1; 1,983,954 bytes, 112 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page). Book error, taught corrected: printed p. 43 claims division “is only possible if the dividend is a multiple of the divisor” (exact division only) — contradicted by Book 3's own division-with-remainder teaching; the course teaches division with remainder. Editorial production notes are printed as page content on printed pp. 17, 18, 21, 24, 25, 28, 30 and 45 — production matter, cited nowhere; the Tema 3 heading is misprinted inside the book (at printed p. 102); PDF pp. 106–112 are blank/back matter apart from the bibliografia (printed p. 107), cited nowhere. The PDF's text layer is massively duplicated (the same text overprinted many times per page) — a production flaw of the file; the content is fully legible and the book was read in full from it.

**Book 5:** *Matemática — Manual da 5.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 5) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Texto Editores, 2021, 2.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-8884-84-8; 4,730,235 bytes, 128 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page). Book errors, taught corrected: printed p. 29's worked example 12 + 37 + 7 + 3 is printed as = 55 with a garbled regrouping (correct total: 59); printed p. 73's equivalence line prints 1/3 = 2/6 = 2/9 (the last term should be 3/9); printed p. 127's median worked example uses an ordered list containing 12, which is not in the exercise's data, and drops one 10 (the median is taught from the data as printed in the exercise: ordered 6, 7, 9, 10, 10, 10, 11, 13, 14 → median 10).

**Book 6:** *Matemática — Manual da 6.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 6) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Texto Editores, 2021, 2.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-8884-85-5; 2,544,284 bytes, 104 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page). Book notes, taught corrected: printed p. 51 first presents the ambiguous expression 28 : 7 × 2 with two candidate readings and tells the pupil to ask the teacher — the rule is fixed by the worked examples on printed p. 52, and the course teaches the standard order (× and ÷ in order, before + and −); printed p. 62's direct-proportionality worked line prints the third ratio with denominator 6 where the sequence's term is 5 (150/5 = 30 — taught from the sequence). The book carries printer's proof marks, and one running footer misprints “Matemática 5ª Classe” (printed p. 99) — production matter, cited nowhere.

## Bahamas added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read the Bahamas' whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **4 documents, all of them Bahamas Ministry of Education curriculum or examination-syllabus documents; 0 pupil (student) textbooks and 0 teacher guides** — and placed 496 requirement items. A curriculum states what is REQUIRED to be taught; it teaches nothing itself. Every Bahamas item in this section is therefore CURRICULUM EVIDENCE — a statement of what the Bahamas requires, worded as a requirement — cited as the evidence kind it is (Book 3's items are examination-SYLLABUS evidence, on the same standing); the Bahamas is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana/Barbados evidence-only precedent, until a Bahamas pupil book is in hand and read. Set correction, as the placements prove it: the expansion map recorded the in-hand set as “4 student + 0 teacher” — the count of 4 is right, the kind is wrong; all four documents are ministry requirement documents (kind = curriculum in both the staging books.json files and textbooks.tsv). The file the records title *BJC Mathematics Curriculum, Grades 7–9* is actually the *Bahamas Junior Certificate 044 Mathematics Syllabus 2003* — a 10-page scanned examination syllabus with no text layer, read visually page by page — and the held Senior High file is the July 2019 revision of the Grades 10–12 guidelines; both are placed as what they are. No pupil textbook or teacher guide is in hand: the pupil books Bahamas schools use are commercial (Hodder *Bahamas Primary Mathematics* Books 1–6 — existence verified in the records, no file held; Books 1–2 name Silver Burdett Ginn Mathematics and Harcourt Math in classroom use, and Book 4's bibliography names STP Caribbean Mathematics, Mathematics: A Complete Course, Fundamental Mathematics for the Caribbean and Nelson Caribbean Mathematics — named only, none held), and the one school-compiled pupil text with an exact link in the records (Akhepran International Academy, *Mathematics BJC Textbook — Grade 7*, 2023) was never downloaded and sits in no record set, so it is not part of this read and is cited nowhere. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct numbered objective in each guidelines book's Scope of Work body — the Content column read with every objective — Book 3 at one item per numbered topic row of its Topics table and Book 4 at one item per CONCEPTS/TOPICS block; an objective restated in full on a continuation page is counted once, at its first full statement, and the two requirements the documents restate inside a second strand (Book 1 Grade 2 estimation, item 69; Book 2 Grade 5 rounding of decimals, item 256) are stocked as the placements place them, each row naming the restatement. The placements' named exclusions — Benchmarks, Scope-and-Sequence charts, pacing guides and Essential Questions in Books 1–2; the Problems of the Day sets, Materials Lists, assessment rubrics, the Children's Literature list and References (record items R1–R7 and R9–R11); Book 3's examination administration, its 19 general aims and its erratum sheet (R8); Book 4's Scope and Sequence charts, Glossary and Technology Integration pages — are stocked nowhere. Book errors found in the read are carried corrected on the rows they touch. Into this lesson the placements stock 22 requirement-evidence items from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3 and Book 4.

**Book 1:** *Primary School Mathematics Curriculum Guidelines, Grades 1–3* (curriculum guidelines) — Bahamas, Ministry of Education, Department of Education, September 2010; 5,558,181 bytes, 263 PDF pages; Scope of Work printed page = PDF page − 51. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — a curriculum states what is required to be taught; it teaches nothing itself.

**Book 2:** *Primary School Mathematics Curriculum Guidelines, Grades 4–6* (curriculum guidelines) — Bahamas, Ministry of Education, Department of Education, September 2010; 5,278,639 bytes, 328 PDF pages; Scope of Work printed page = PDF page − 54. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — a curriculum states what is required to be taught; it teaches nothing itself.

**Book 3:** *Bahamas Junior Certificate 044 Mathematics Syllabus 2003* (examination syllabus) — Bahamas, Ministry of Education, Youth & Sports, Testing & Evaluation Section; 2,351,047 bytes, 10 PDF pages (a scanned-image PDF with no text layer, read visually page by page); printed page = PDF page − 1. The library records title this file “BJC Mathematics Curriculum, Grades 7–9” — the held file is the 2003 examination syllabus, and it is placed as what it is (set correction, stated here; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records). SYLLABUS EVIDENCE ONLY — an examination syllabus states what candidates are required to know; it teaches nothing itself.

**Book 4:** *Senior High School Mathematics Curriculum Guidelines, Grades 10–12* (curriculum guidelines; the held file is the July 2019 revision, footer “High School Mathematics Curriculum – The Bahamas, 2019”) — Bahamas, Department of Education; 3,766,723 bytes, 219 PDF pages; printed page = PDF page. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — a curriculum states what is required to be taught; it teaches nothing itself.

## Belize added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Belize's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **3 documents, all of them Belize Ministry of Education *Mathematics Curriculum: Teacher's Guide* books (August 2012) — Infant 1, Standard 1 and Standard IV; 0 pupil (student) textbooks** — and placed 108 teaching items. Every document in the set is a teacher's guide: it states the learning outcomes pupils must reach and teaches the teacher how to teach each one (comments, sample lesson objectives, specific content, sample activities and assessment). Every Belize item in this section is therefore TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE — Belize's guides teach teachers how to teach the topic; no Belize pupil textbook is in hand — cited as the evidence kind it is; Belize is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until a Belize pupil book is in hand and read. No pupil Maths book is in hand: the pupil books on the official National Textbook Program list (*My BZ Math* Infant 1–Standard 4, Macmillan; *Caribbean Primary Math* Bk 6 for Standard 5, Hodder; *Active Math* for Standard 6, Macmillan) are all recorded missing in the library files, and the guides' own textbook linkages point to the legacy pupil series *Primary Mathematics for Belize* (Hodder, by term codes Y1T1…/S1T1…), which is named by the guides only — not in hand, not read, and not a course source. Three further Maths documents — the *Primary curriculum: Mathematics* division webs (Lower Division, Middle Division, Upper Division) — are recorded at exact link only, never downloaded and in no books.json; they are not part of this read and are cited nowhere. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct numbered learning outcome in each guide's Standards and Learning Outcomes section (35 outcomes in each guide, 105 in all) plus 3 items taught in a detailed section but stated in no outcome of that guide (items 64, 65 and 87, marked D); outcome numbering is continuous across the series (Infant 1 carries M1.1–M20.1, Standard 1 continues at M1.10, Standard IV at M1.16), which is why the codes do not restart at 1 in the later guides. Printed page = PDF page in all three guides. The placements' named exclusions — the guides' front matter (programme philosophy: goals, rationale, assessment discussion), strand introductions and Big Ideas prose, sample lesson objectives, sample assessments, sample activities and print/technology resource columns as apparatus, the shared manipulatives list, the websites lists, the shared Mathematics Glossary (carried in the placements as record item R2 — a teacher reference list, cited nowhere as content), and the standards each guide marks N/A at its level (Book 1: M2 place value and M13 equations; Book 2: M14 measurement concepts; Book 3: M5 position/ranking numbers and M14) — are stocked nowhere. The 14 book errors and inconsistencies found in the read (placements E1–E14) are carried corrected on the rows they touch: item 35 is placed as data interpretation, not probability, though the guide files it under its probability standard (E5); item 51's set language is taught with “complement” for the book's “compliment” (E6); item 66's many-to-one correspondence is taught as one symbol or unit standing for many objects, not the guide's function-style definition (E8); item 82 is stocked WITHOUT the outcome's fourth bullet (“expand powers with same base to add or subtract”), which is not a valid add/subtract procedure (E9); item 81 is placed as stated and flagged as vague and advanced for its level (E10); item 104 is placed as measurement and taught with the standard measurement meanings, the guide's own definition of precision being a data-management sense (E13); and item 106 follows the guide's own correct definitions — its assessment text's “medium” for median is an error (E12). Into this lesson the placements stock 5 teacher-guide-evidence items from Book 1, Book 2 and Book 3.

**Book 1:** *Mathematics Curriculum: Teacher's Guide, Infant 1* (teacher guide, Grade 1) — Belize, Ministry of Education, August 2012; 738,164 bytes, 58 PDF pages. Standards and Learning Outcomes section printed pp. 12–16; detailed teaching sections printed pp. 17–52. Printed page = PDF page. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide teaches the teacher how to teach the topic; it is not a pupil textbook.

**Book 2:** *Mathematics Curriculum: Teacher's Guide, Standard 1* (teacher guide, Grade 3) — Belize, Ministry of Education, August 2012; 803,827 bytes, 62 PDF pages. Standards and Learning Outcomes section printed pp. 12–16; detailed teaching sections printed pp. 17–56. Printed page = PDF page. Outcome numbering continues the series from Book 1 (M1.10 onwards). TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide teaches the teacher how to teach the topic; it is not a pupil textbook.

**Book 3:** *Mathematics Curriculum: Teacher's Guide, Standard IV* (teacher guide, Grade 6) — Belize, Ministry of Education, August 2012; 868,323 bytes, 51 PDF pages. Standards and Learning Outcomes section printed pp. 12–15; detailed teaching sections printed pp. 16–45. Printed page = PDF page. Outcome numbering continues the series (M1.16 onwards). The detailed sections of this guide are markedly thinner than in Books 1–2 (several state “Specific Content: N/A”); placements rest on the outcomes as stated. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide teaches the teacher how to teach the topic; it is not a pupil textbook.

## Australia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Australia's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **9 books, all of them student textbooks** (Years 8–10: eight Cambridge volumes — the Essential Mathematics Australian Curriculum, Victorian Curriculum and CambridgeMATHS NSW lines, plus the GOLD support edition and the CORE pathway volume — and one Jacaranda volume, Maths Quest 10+10A) — and placed 844 teaching items. Australia has no government textbook series (ACARA writes the Australian Curriculum but publishes and prescribes no textbooks); the set is the school-used commercial series the country records name, and every book is cited as such. All 9 files are distinct works (distinct ISBNs, extents and contents, verified per book on the read); the publisher-line relations are stated in the placements: Book A1 (GOLD) is the support edition of Book A2's Year 8 material, Book A3 is the same line in its Victorian ©2024 edition, Books A4 and A6 are the NSW syllabus editions, and Book A9 is Book A8's CORE pathway companion. In the sections below the books are cited as Books A1–A9. Australia COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every item is pupil-textbook teaching. The read's four flagged claims are carried exactly: (F1) Book A3's contents lists its Chapter 11, Algorithmic thinking, as “(online only)”, but the file in hand contains the full chapter, bound after the Answers (printed pp. 926–941) — it is read and placed, and it founds this course's algorithmic-thinking lesson; (F2) Book A7's contents lists a Chapter 18, Algorithmic thinking, with no page numbers, and the chapter is NOT in the file (searched end to end; the book closes with its Glossary, printed p. 1106, and Index, printed p. 1117) — recorded as absent, not read, and no item rests on it; (F3) Book A9's running head at printed p. 644 prints section 10E's code as “0E” — the book's own production error; the placement cites 10E, as the section's heading and contents give it; (F4) only the 9 Maths books are this read's set — the country records' wider Australia picture (12 staged books across subjects, plus filled-by-exact-link titles) is not merged into this count, and in particular the two senior Maths titles that exist only as exact-link records (Cambridge Senior Specialist Mathematics, QCE Units 3&4, 2e, and Cambridge Senior Mathematical Methods, QCE Units 3&4, 2e) are NOT in hand and were NOT read. The placements' named exclusions — front matter and “how to use this book” guides; every chapter's pre-tests, progress quizzes, unlettered applications / problem-solving / modelling / investigation spreads, puzzles and games, chapter summaries, checklists and reviews, semester reviews, glossaries, indexes and answer sections — are apparatus/assessment, teach no new content, and are stocked nowhere. The set is Years 8–10 secondary only, so the primary lessons (counting, place value, first steps) receive no Australia item — that is the set's shape, not a reading gap. Australia's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand book (9/9) has been read and placed; the two senior QCE exact-link titles do not block the in-hand class (if exact-link titles are ever ruled in-scope for the courses reckoning, Australia drops to PARTIAL until they are grabbed and read). Into this lesson the placements stock 4 items from Book A1, Book A2, Book A3, Book A4.

**Book A1:** *Cambridge Mathematics 8 (CambridgeMaths GOLD)* — Australia, Greenwood et al., Cambridge University Press, ©2012, ISBN 978-1-107-64383-3; 62,774,078 bytes, 554 PDF pp. The GOLD support edition of the Essential Mathematics Year 8 line, developed for students who require additional support in mathematics; a distinct work from Book A2 (different ISBN, extent and arrangement), not a duplicate file. Pages cited are printed pages (printed = PDF − 14).

**Book A2:** *Essential Mathematics for the Australian Curriculum Year 8, Third Edition* — Australia, Greenwood et al., Cambridge University Press, ©2019, ISBN 978-1-108-77281-5; 35,833,730 bytes, 846 PDF pp. The main Year 8 Australian Curriculum volume of the Essential Mathematics line (Book A1 is its Gold support sibling). Pages cited are printed pages (printed = PDF − 18).

**Book A3:** *Essential Mathematics for the Victorian Curriculum Year 8, Third Edition* — Australia, Greenwood et al., Cambridge University Press, ©2024, ISBN 978-1-009-48075-8; 24,871,876 bytes, 955 PDF pp. The Victorian Curriculum state edition of the Essential Mathematics Year 8 line — a distinct work from Book A2. Pages cited are printed pages (printed = PDF − 20). Its contents lists Chapter 11, Algorithmic thinking, as “(online only)”, but the full chapter IS bound into this file, after the Answers (printed pp. 926–941), and is read and placed (flag F1).

**Book A4:** *CambridgeMATHS NSW Stage 4 (Year 8), Second Edition* — Australia, Palmer, McDaid, Greenwood et al., Cambridge University Press, ©2018, ISBN 978-1-108-46627-1; 27,138,098 bytes, 680 PDF pp. The NSW syllabus edition for Stage 4 Year 8. Pages cited are printed pages (printed = PDF − 16). It has no probability chapter — its statistics chapter is data-only; that is the volume's real shape (its contents were read in full), not a missing part of the file.

## Bhutan added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Bhutan's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **16 files: 13 student books and 3 teacher's guides of the Royal Government of Bhutan's *Understanding Mathematics* series (Department of School Education / Ministry of Education and Skills Development), English-medium throughout** — and placed 657 pupil teaching items and 30 teacher-evidence items. **Set correction, proved by the read: the expansion map's count of 10 student + 3 teacher is WRONG — the map counted only the textbooks.tsv snapshot (PP, I, II, IV–X) and missed the Class III, XI and XII student books recorded as secured and pushed in the staging books.json files and the push state; the true in-hand set is 13 student + 3 teacher = 16 files, every one grabbed and verified (byte size on every file; MD5 against books.json on the 11 files that carry one — all match; page counts match every record) and read in full.** The Class I staging-path collision is resolved: the main-branch grade-01 file is the student activity book (Book 3); the Class I teacher's guide (Book 4) is a separate, MD5-verified file that books.json records under the same staging path name. **Critical filing flag, carried exactly: both BHSEC filings claim "complete book, not a sample/preview" — FALSE. The Class XI file holds only chapters 1–5 of the 26 its own contents lists and the Class XII file only chapters 1–4 of the 19 its contents lists; only the chapters actually present were placed, and both books are cited as the partial excerpts they are.** The 3 teacher's guides are teacher evidence only: their items are named below as teacher evidence and are never counted as pupil teaching. The PP activity book's Reproducible Pages (printed pp. 89–116, cut-out masters) and Summative Assessment Recording Sheets (pp. 117–132) are apparatus and are stocked nowhere; its assessment sheets renumber two chapters against the body (Data Management is Ch. 6 there, Ch. 9 in the body; Ordinal Numbers and Halves Ch. 11 there, Ch. 10 in the body) — body numbering is used throughout. The Class 1 activity book's Reproducible Pages (printed pp. 75–80) and Summative Assessment Recording Sheets (pp. 81–100) are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The Class 2 activity book's Summative Assessment Recording Sheets (printed pp. 99–118) are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The pupil Chapter 9 pages open at Lesson 3 (Interpreting Bar Graphs) — no pupil Lessons 1–2 for that chapter appear in the file; the guide's parallel survey and data-collection lessons stand as teacher evidence instead. Book errors in the Class 2 book are carried corrected: its imprint prints the copyright year as "20234", read as 2023 as on its guide, and its Chapter 6 Lesson 6 tells pupils to draw a cylinder and label its base and apex — a cylinder has no apex; the apex belongs to the cone in the same lesson. The Class 3 textbook's Chapter Overviews, Chapter Reviews and course-objectives/assessment front matter are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The Class IV textbook's Getting Started spreads, GAME and CONNECTIONS features, Unit Revisions, Glossary, Measurement Reference and Answers are apparatus/practice and are stocked nowhere. The Class V textbook's Getting Started spreads, GAME and CONNECTIONS features, Unit Revisions, Glossary, Measurement Reference and Answers are apparatus/practice and are stocked nowhere. The Class VI textbook's Getting Started spreads, GAME and CONNECTIONS features, Unit Revisions, Glossary, Measurement Reference and Answers are apparatus/practice and are stocked nowhere. Its contents numbers two different sections 6.2.2 ("Bisectors" and "EXPLORE: Sorting Quadrilaterals"); the second is cited with the duplication named. The Class VII textbook's Getting Started spreads, GAME and CONNECTIONS features, Unit Revisions, Glossary, Measurement Reference and Answers are apparatus/practice and are stocked nowhere. Into this lesson the placements stock 21 items (20 pupil + 1 teacher-evidence) from Books 1, 3, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10 and 11.

Set boundary, proved from the records: `textbooks.tsv` carries exactly 17 Cabo Verde rows (all Government, grades 1–7), of which exactly three are Mathematics, all student; the download list’s §C enumerates 93 ministry manuals, including Maths manuals for grades 1, 2, 4, 6, 8, 9, 10 and 11 and applied-Maths manuals — every one of those is exact-link-only (no TSV row, no staging file, no GitHub copy) and NOT in hand, so none is cited here; counting any of them would be a set error. Record gap, reported not fixed (record-side): `phase3-push-state.json` contains no Cabo Verde entry at all, and there is no Cabo Verde folder or `books.json` in phase3-staging — the in-hand evidence is the TSV’s GitHub URLs themselves, and all three GitHub copies were located, verified (byte size and MD5; the Grade 5 ministry copy is byte-identical to the GitHub copy, so that book is one book, cited once) and read directly, so the set is settled despite the push-state gap. No duplicates exist in the set: three distinct grades, three distinct books. Printed page = PDF page in all three books. The placements’ named exclusions — front matter, unit openers, objectives lists and the closing national-anthem page in the Grade 5 and Grade 7 books; the Grade 3 book’s cover plate and welcome/mascot pages (PDF pp. 1, 3, 5), its part-opener pages (PDF pp. 55, 85, 107, 111), its closing test “Teste” (printed pp. 125–129 — mixed assessment re-using taught content, no new teaching) and the flag / national-anthem page (PDF p. 130) — and pure exercise / consolidation blocks that teach nothing new are stocked nowhere. The 12 book errors found in the read (2 in the Grade 5 book, 5 in the Grade 7 book, 5 in the Grade 3 book) are carried corrected on the rows they touch. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 item from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Manual de Matemática 3º ano* (student manual) — Cabo Verde, Ministério da Educação, Edição 2019; authors Jimmy Leppãnen, Sara Hoye, FvZ; editor Gamlin & Zeipel Communication AB; 140,731,505 bytes, 130 PDF pp., MD5 112ca4f2fc6577b4eb035b0237d9253c (read from the GitHub release copy in release `full-quality-oversized-books`; its byte size equals the ministry HEAD size recorded in the download list §C row 13 exactly). ## Canada added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Canada's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **2 works in 4 files: *Math First Peoples — Teacher Resource Guide, Elementary & Secondary* (FNESC/FNSA, ©2020), 273 pp. — the one book in Canada's classified in-hand Maths set (0 student + 1 teacher) — and the borderline pupil work *I Like Math!* (Government of Nunavut, 2017), one Level 8 guided-reading fiction book held in three language editions of 11 pp. each and filed in every record under the Inuktut Ilinniaqta Early Books label, not under Maths** — and placed 117 items (112 teacher-guide evidence items from the guide, items 1–112, plus 5 pupil items from *I Like Math!*, items S1–S5). Every item from the guide is TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE: the guide directs teachers how to teach BC mathematics through First Peoples contexts, with mini-units, activities and blackline masters, and states BC curriculum connections in its tables; it expressly does not explain the mathematics or the curriculum in detail (its printed p. 35) and presumes a trained teacher. No pupil Maths textbook is in hand for Canada: Canada has no national textbook series (education is provincial/territorial), and the five provincial student textbooks in the records (Nelson *Principles of Mathematics 10*; McGraw-Hill Ryerson *Principles of Mathematics 9*; Pearson *Math Makes Sense 3, 7, 8*) stand filled-by-exact-link only — never staged, never secured, not read here and cited nowhere (a sixth, *Nelson Mathematics 8*, is a candidate only, its imprint never read). Canada is therefore cited as EVIDENCE ONLY, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent: Canada is added to this lesson's countries line and named in its rows, but counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure. The *I Like Math!* items are stocked as pupil evidence from that work exactly as the placements label them — 3 carry taught content (S1, S2, S4) and 2 are context/named-only (S3, S5) — and are likewise counted in no taught-in figure; whether the Nunavut early-books titles count as pupil Maths at all is a record-side question for the parent, reported here, not ruled on. The guide's Water-Keepers items (98–105) rest on the guide's printed teaching alone: the separate FNESC Water Keepers digital resource the guide points to (printed p. 224) is in no record as in hand and was not read. The guide is the 2020 revision of the 2011 *Math First Peoples* resource; the 2011 predecessor located on a Padlet host is not a separate in-hand book and is cited nowhere. The placements' named exclusions — the guide's introduction, goals/rationale and teacher-reflection material; First Peoples Principles of Learning, story protocols and respectful-practice rules; the resource-design rubric; community-visit apparatus beyond the Interview Guide's six content categories; unit-organization front matter; the cultural/historical context narratives and stories inside the mini-units (context-setting only; no story text is reproduced anywhere in the placements or here); BLM worksheets that merely repeat an itemised activity; formative/summative assessment prompts, guiding questions, video and website lists and the Additional Resources bibliographies; and the *I Like Math!* front/back matter — teach no new mathematics and are stocked nowhere. The 5 book errors found in the read (placements E1–E5) are carried corrected on the rows they touch: E1 (sphere-volume worked example, printed p. 123 — a unit slip and a garbled intermediate line inside a volume calculation; the final answer, 500 cm³, is correct) on item 56; E2 (BLM 6 conversion sheet, printed p. 185 — the percent-to-decimal rule states the decimal-point direction backwards; both printed examples are correct) on item 83; E3 (Moose Tracks answer key, printed p. 181 — mixes “less than or equal to 20” with m < 20 and marks the boundary value inconsistently) on item 71; E4 (Circle Unit Answer Key, printed p. 211 — says “smoke hole” for “fire circle”; the project instructions at printed p. 204 state it correctly) on item 92; E5 (Weaving BLM list, printed p. 55 — numbers both Keep or Toss sheets BLM 16; they are BLM 15 and BLM 16) is a list typo affecting no item's content. Printed page = PDF page in the guide throughout; *I Like Math!* citations are its printed story folios. Canada's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 item (item 50).

**Book 1:** *Math First Peoples — Teacher Resource Guide, Elementary & Secondary* (teacher resource guide) — Canada, First Nations Education Steering Committee (FNESC) and First Nations Schools Association (FNSA), ©2020; 32,264,979 bytes (byte-exact against the record), 273 PDF pages. Printed page = PDF page throughout. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide directs teachers how to teach BC mathematics through First Peoples contexts and states curriculum connections; it expressly does not explain the mathematics in detail (printed p. 35); it is not a pupil textbook.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| The buried part of a pole is generally one fifth to one sixth of its length, and determining that part for a class pole | Canada — *Math First Peoples — Teacher Resource Guide* (FNESC/FNSA, 2020) (teacher guide) — teacher-guide evidence, printed p. 112 |

## Costa Rica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Costa Rica's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **25 books, all of them student books: the five CONED distance-secondary antologías *Antología de Matemáticas*, Niveles 7–11 (UNED/CONED — Nivel Sétimo 2017, Nivel Noveno 2018, Nivel 11 2019; Nivel Décimo by MSc. Jorge Díaz Porras) and the ITCR Proyecto EVEPRIM primary books for Grades 3 and 6 (10 textos + 10 folletos de prácticas across Relaciones y Álgebra, Números, Geometría, Medidas, Estadística and Probabilidad); 0 teacher guides for Costa Rica Maths exist in hand** — and placed 229 teaching items. **SET CORRECTION: the expansion map's count of 17 student is WRONG** — 17 is exactly the number of Costa Rica Mathematics rows in textbooks.tsv (10 EVEPRIM textos + only 2 of the 10 EVEPRIM folletos + 5 CONED antologías); the TSV was never updated for 8 folletos staged on 2026-10-25, and the staging books.json files and phase3-push-state.json agree on the true set of 25 (the folletos count student-side, per the map's own stated basis). All 25 were grabbed, verified (byte size + MD5 against books.json; the two Relaciones y Álgebra textos, which have no books.json entry, verified by size + title page as the main-repo files) and read in full — no partial excerpts, no duplicates (the SharePoint folder copies of the six texto books matched the staged sizes byte-for-byte, so they are the same files, cited once) and no unopenable files. The three Grade 3 textos were read in their November 2026 editions, the in-hand editions per books.json; the GitHub release mid-size-books-3 still holds the superseded October editions of those three textos (a record-level flag, reported, not fixed — same books, older exports; only the November editions are cited here). Two chapter promises in the CONED books are NOT teaching and place no item anywhere: CONED 7's Capítulo 4 is titled Estadística y Probabilidad but teaches statistics only (probability appears only in its introductory discussion questions, pp. 209–214), and CONED 10's Capítulo 1 objectives promise plane transformations that no Tema in the chapter teaches (only a recall of Grade 8 homothety). The placements' named exclusions — front matter, indices, bibliographies and answer keys (soluciones); the EVEPRIM answer-key chapters and the review chapters that re-teach already-placed sections; the CONED chapter motto/introduction pages and “desafío matemático” pages; and the videos and web interactives the EVEPRIM books link to — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The 5 CONED book-error groups found in the read — CONED 7: the angle table (p. 106) labels a 360° angle “CONVEXO”, the inverse-proportionality example (pp. 201–203) states v·t = 247.5 for a product of 330 and “very close to 3 hours” at 78 km/h for ≈4.23 h with garbled fraction work, the triangle inequality (p. 125) omits “any two” sides, the median answer (p. 238) gives 68 for a median of 67, the worked powers page (p. 64) misaligns its results, and a tiling exercise (p. 35) reads “largo por largo”; CONED 8: the table of contents numbers the equations Tema “Tema 4” while the body heads it “Tema 5” (numbering slip only); CONED 9: the SI prefix table (p. 74) gives exa the symbol “R” (exa is E), the radian-to-degree rule (p. 117) is printed inverted/garbled though its worked examples are correct, and the contents duplicates “Función Cuadrática” for Capítulo 3 Temas 1 and 4 and mislabels Capítulo 4 as “Capítulo III”; CONED 10: the falling-body model (p. 373) prints “12” for ½ (the worked values use ½ correctly), the key (p. 42) duplicates “tangentes” where “secantes” is meant, p. 14 asks for the area of a “circunferencia” (the circle), and the pentagon solution (p. 86) drops its degree symbols; CONED 11: the logarithm table (p. 118) misaligns its examples (it prints log₅ 5 = 2, which belongs to log₅ 25), the wound-model solution (p. 172) shows an unsigned division display before the correct result, and “invectiva” (p. 121) is a typo for inyectiva — are carried corrected on the rows they touch. One further note is carried as the placements carry it: the Medidas G3 answer key's building-height estimate reads 45 dam in the October copy and “4 dam” in the November extraction — treated as a re-export/extraction artefact in the key, not teaching content, and cited nowhere. Costa Rica's books are pupil books, so Costa Rica is COUNTED: it joins this lesson's acceptance count wherever the placements stock an item. CONED printed page = PDF page throughout; EVEPRIM pages are cited by PDF page with the book's section numbers (two exceptions print folios: the EVEPRIM 6 Probabilidad texto's printed folio = PDF page − 6 and the EVEPRIM 3 Medidas texto's = PDF page − 7; citations give PDF pages). Into this lesson the placements stock 5 items (items 27, 118, 143, 144 and 227).

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Bulgaria's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **5 files = 4 works, all pupil textbooks: the Бит и Техника (Bit i Tekhnika) *Математика* for Grades 1, 2 and 3 and for Grade 4 in two part-files (1 част / 2 част), 642 PDF pages in all** — and placed 123 teaching items. **The expansion map's “5 student + 0 teacher” is CORRECT as a file count (5 files, 4 works). Full-book vs sample verdict: NO in-hand Maths file is a sample** — every file's extent matches its own imprint's print-sheet count (Grade 1: 20.5 sheets, 128 lessons to printed p. 158 + 12 appendices; Grade 2: 18 sheets, 112 lessons to printed p. 140; Grade 3: 18 sheets, 112 lessons to printed p. 140; Grade 4: 10 + 11.5 sheets, lessons 1–136), and the 8 proven publisher samples in Bulgaria's library record (Music G6, Foreign Language G6/G7, БЕЛ G10/G11 and Geography G5) contain no Maths book, so the library's sample ruling does not touch this set. All five PDFs are image-only with no usable text layer and were read visually from page renders (cover, imprint, the book's own contents, and body pages for every distinct topic); printed folio = PDF page − 1 in every book, and Grade 4 Part 2 restarts its own pagination (its citations are marked “Part 2”). No Bulgaria Maths book was in the course before this merge — the string “Bulgaria” occurred nowhere under `maths/` — so every placed item is new here. Set boundary: everything else Bulgarian Maths in the records is exact-link / reader only and NOT in hand — Riva Maths G1–G4 PDFs (duplicate slots for the same grades), Klett epub Maths G5/G6 readers, Klett FlipHTML5 Maths G7/G8 (fullness unproven) and Архимед Maths G10/G11 Ourboox PDFs — none is part of this read and none is cited here; if any is later secured and read, Bulgaria reverts to PARTIAL until it is stocked. The placements' named exclusions — each grade's diagnostics, self-checks and consolidation lessons, drills, blitz tournaments, games and projects (other than the Grade 4 data project stocked in the statistics lessons), end-of-unit and year reviews, and the Grade 1 book's 12 cut-out appendices (number cards, shape cut-outs, play money, tangram, clock face, addition wheel, 0–20 ruler) — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere; the topics they practise are counted at the lessons that teach them. **Book-internal flag, carried exactly: the Grade 1 book's introduction (printed p. 4) promises the unit litre, but no lesson teaches it — the contents list no litre lesson and the closing units summary (lesson 127, printed p. 155, read on the body page) lists only hour, euro, eurocent, kilogram and centimetre — so the litre is stocked nowhere, and Bulgaria Grade 1 stands as teaching cm, kg, euro/eurocent and the hour, not the litre.** Book errors proper (wrong mathematics) were found nowhere in the pages read; contents-page slips (e.g. Grade 4 Part 1 listing its angle lessons out of order in the contents) are apparatus slips, not teaching errors, and are counted nowhere. The placements' one NEW LESSON NEEDED topic — calculator use (Grade 4 Part 2, lessons 118–119, item 121) — is stocked in the existing `money-and-time/calculator-use` lesson built by the Sri Lanka expansion merge; no second calculator lesson is built. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 items (item 92).

The document is a teacher's guide: for every lesson of the matching pupil manuel it states the headline objective, the pedagogical intention, the pupil objectives, the lesson conduct, the activities and the worked answers. Every Djibouti item in this section is therefore TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE — the guide teaches teachers how to teach the topic; it is never a pupil textbook — cited as the evidence kind it is; Djibouti is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until a Djibouti pupil book is in hand and read. No pupil Maths book is in hand: the guide states (printed pp. 9–10) that since the 2019 school year teachers and pupils have a matching pupil manuel and livret of the same series (« À MOI LES MATHS », 5ème année), organised in the same four eight-week modules — those pupil books exist but are not in hand (placements record item R1), the guide's references to the manuel's pages are apparatus, not pupil content read here, and the student Maths files the library records locate are link-only partial extracts, never downloaded; none is part of this read and none is cited. Printed page = PDF page throughout the guide. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught content stated in a lesson sheet's headline objective and pupil objectives, splitting a sheet where it teaches two contents that belong to different course lessons; the pedagogical apparatus around each sheet (situation conduct, activity-by-activity commentary, exercise commentaries and synthesis questions) is not re-counted per activity. The placements' named exclusions — the avant-propos, préambule and présentation (printed pp. 3, 8–12: the competency approach, the four “moments of study”, the teacher's role, evaluation and remediation apparatus), the sommaire itself (printed pp. 4–7; its faults are placements record items R2 and R4), the mental-calculation routines (PLM) opening most sheets, per-sheet conduct and commentary counted once per content, and the guide's cross-references to the pupil manuel and livret pages — are stocked nowhere; the one content-bearing statement in the front matter, the end-of-primary objective's domain list (printed p. 9), is placed as a programme item in the ratio-and-proportion lesson. The Module 2 sheet at printed pp. 55–56 is a near-verbatim reprint of the Module 1 sheet at printed pp. 14–15 (placements record item R3); its content is placed once, as item 1. Into this lesson the placements stock 3 teacher-guide-evidence items from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher guide, Grade 5) — Djibouti, CRIPEN (Ministère de l'Éducation Nationale et de la Formation Professionnelle), édition 2023, series « À MOI LES MATHS »; 1,494,038 bytes, 129 PDF pages. Printed page = PDF page throughout. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide teaches the teacher how to teach the topic; it is not a pupil textbook.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| Common fractions for the first time: identifying and carrying out fair shares of a unit, writing the fraction for a coloured part, numerator and denominator, and placing fractions on a graduated line | Djibouti — *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher guide) — teacher-guide evidence, printed pp. 82–83 |
| Framing a fraction between two consecutive whole numbers, using the multiplication table of the denominator | Djibouti — *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher guide) — teacher-guide evidence, printed pp. 88–89 |
| Writing a (decimal) fraction as the sum of a whole number and a fraction smaller than 1, as the step towards decimal writing | Djibouti — *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher guide) — teacher-guide evidence, printed pp. 95–96 |

Djibouti enters this lesson as teacher-guide evidence only: it is named in the countries line and in the rows above, but it is NOT added to the acceptance count above — the only document in Djibouti's in-hand set is a CRIPEN teacher's guide (Grade 5, édition 2023), not a pupil textbook, cited as the evidence kind it is, never counted as pupil teaching (the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent). Djibouti's class for Maths is PARTIAL (teacher-evidence-only): the 1 in-hand book is read in full and stocked, but no pupil textbook is in hand — the matching « À MOI LES MATHS » Grade 5 pupil manuel and livret exist and are named by the guide itself, but no complete pupil Maths file is held, and the located student Maths files are link-only partial extracts, unread.

## Eritrea added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Eritrea's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **12 books, all student textbooks: Mathematics, Grades 1–12, one book per grade (State of Eritrea, Ministry of Education; Grades 1–5 Tigrinya-medium, Grades 6–12 English), 2,029 PDF pages in total** — and placed 256 teaching items. The expansion map's “12 student + 0 teacher” is exactly right: download-lists/eritrea.md (Telegram hunt 2026-10-25), the staging books.json files, textbooks.tsv and phase3-push-state.json all give the same twelve distinct student textbooks and nothing else; every file's byte size and pdfinfo page count match its record exactly, and every title/copyright page names the State of Eritrea Ministry of Education. No Eritrean Maths teacher guide is in hand (none exists in any record), so every Eritrea row in this course is a pupil-textbook row — there are no teacher-evidence rows, and Eritrea counts in every lesson it stocks. Byte-identical second copies of the Grade 2 and Grade 3 books sit in the hunt folder (MD5-verified) — one book each, cited once. The one teacher-guide-shaped Maths file in the hunt folder is Ethiopian (Grade 6, GEQIP) — not an Eritrean book, excluded from the set entirely and cited nowhere. All twelve books are complete: every book runs to the end of its own contents. The placements' named exclusions — the introductions/prefaces of every book (learner-centred pedagogy addresses, no subject content), and exercises, activities and revision question sets as apparatus (their topics are counted through the sections that teach them, never double-counted) — are stocked nowhere. Printed-page bases differ per book and are stated in each book entry below. Into this lesson the placements stock 6 items from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3, Book 4 and Book 5.

## Finland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Finland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **24 files / 23 distinct works of government-hosted open material (OPH and the aoe.fi Library of Open Educational Resources); Finland has NO national textbook series** — and placed 173 items (51 pupil + 122 teacher evidence). The records' kind labels are wrong for most of the set: 13 of the 18 Mathematics-labelled “student” files are actually teacher-facing (in-service course books, theory texts, activity series and instructor guides), and only 6 files are pupil-facing (the Metsämatikkaa cards, the quadrilateral chart, the weather game, the Päivänselvää chapter, the programming workbook and its model solutions) — every book below is carried at its TRUE kind, never the record label. One byte-identical duplicate (aoe.2674-alku = aoe.2686-alku, the same file under two aoe records) is cited once; the Päivänselvää book is a Chapter-2-only excerpt, cited only for what it holds; the quadrilateral chart's filed copy is 1 content page + 175 blank padding pages. The placements' named exclusions — the weather game's non-temperature content, the computing-pedagogy matter in the two Turku course books, the motivation and mindset material in the JoMa books, the Pikkumatikoita parts 2, 8 and 11, and the programming books' pure-programming tasks — are stocked nowhere. Pupil items are COUNTED normally; teacher-evidence items are named in the rows and stay outside the acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 2 pupil items and 2 teacher-evidence items from Book 1, Book 2 and Book 3.

TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — an OPH supplementary teacher activity collection, expressly “not tied to any textbook”; Finland has no national textbook series.

Printed page = PDF page (each card numbered). PUPIL material — counted.

**Book 3:** *Perusmatematiikkaa ohjelmoimalla* (pupil workbook, aoe.1371) — Finland, Järvenpää, 2021, ISBN 978-952-94-4547-9; 3,056,839 bytes, 62 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page. ## Dominican Republic added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read the Dominican Republic's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **14 books, all of them student books (0 teacher guides exist in hand)**: the 11 dedicated *Matemática* books of MINERD's Proyecto Libro Abierto, Serie 1 (*Matemática* 2°–6° Primaria and *Matemática* 1°–6° Secundaria), plus the mathematics content of the 3 integrated *Primero* volumes (Grade 1 has no dedicated *Matemática* book; only the three volumes' Matemática blocks are placed) — 3,184 PDF pages in all — and placed 661 teaching items (614 from the dedicated books, 47 from the Primero volumes). Every book was grabbed fresh by the authenticated GitHub API route, verified byte-exact against its staging `books.json` byte count and cover/title-proved, and the grabbed copy deleted after reading; the recorded Google Drive fallback is login-walled from this host and delivered no book. The expansion map's “16 student” count is wrong as a count of *Matemática* books: the dedicated set is 11, and 16 is reachable only by adding integrated all-subject files that are not *Matemática* books. Printed page = PDF page in every book (verified on the unit dividers and page footers). The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught topic — a named section or a coherent teaching block inside a unit. The placements' named exclusions — the apparatus of all 14 books (unit-opening, group-activity, evaluation, self-assessment, bibliography and book-care pages, except the five group activities that teach a topic found nowhere else in their book, which are placed); the *Primero Volumen I* Matemática diagnostic evaluation; the Primero competency pages; the Primero volumes' non-maths sequences (with two maths-adjacent blocks inside them named in the placements and stocked nowhere); the 5 Nivel Inicial integrated books (a scope note, not part of the Maths set); and one record artefact that is not a book (a stray partial-upload `.part` file in the grade-04 push state) — are stocked nowhere. One book error is carried corrected on the row it touches: *Matemática 3° Primaria*'s weight table (evaluation, printed p. 152; the same table family at p. 147) pairs 8 lb with 48 oz — wrong: 1 lb = 16 oz, so 8 lb = 128 oz (48 oz is 3 lb). Minor slips, no action: the contents lists of *Matemática* 2° and 3° Secundaria run one page behind the printed folios — every citation uses the folios. Checked and NOT an error: *Matemática 4° Secundaria*, printed p. 84, prints Pythagoras as a² = b² + c² with a defined as the hypotenuse — correct as printed. In the sections below the books are cited as Books 1–14 in the placements' order. The Dominican Republic COUNTS in a lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of these student-book rows. The Dominican Republic's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Maths book (the 11 dedicated *Matemática* books plus the Primero volumes carrying Grade 1's maths) has been read in full and placed.

Into this lesson the placements stock 13 items from Book 2, Book 3, Book 4, Book 5 and Book 6.

**Book 2:** *Matemática 3° Primaria* (student book, Libro Abierto Serie 1) — Dominican Republic, MINERD (Ministerio de Educación), Proyecto Libro Abierto; 83,680,456 bytes, 176 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page.

## Gabon added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Gabon's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **17 documents: 6 pupil textbooks (the CIAM *Mathématiques* volumes for 6e, 5e, 4e, 2e Scientifique, 1re SM and 1re Littéraire, EDICEF), 6 teacher guides (the *Super Efficace* guides pédagogiques for 1re–5e année, EDIG/EDICEF, and the CIAM *Mathématiques 2e S* guide pédagogique) and 5 curriculum documents (the IPN *Curriculum Mathématiques* for 1re–5e année)** — every file grabbed fresh for the read, its byte size and page count matching the records exactly and its identity proved from cover/title/sommaire pages — and placed 522 items: 83 pupil-textbook teaching items, 389 teacher-evidence items and 50 curriculum-requirement items. The expansion map's total of 17 files is right but its split is wrong in kind (the read's flagged claim F1): the map lists “11 student + 6 teacher”, counting the 5 IPN curriculum PDFs among the student books; the true split is 6 pupil textbooks + 6 teacher guides + 5 curriculum documents. **No *Super Efficace* pupil book is in hand at any primary grade** — the primary pupil books are print-only commercial (EDIG) and were never secured, so Gabon's primary coverage is the IPN curriculum plus the teacher guide for each year; each guide teaches every lesson of the absent pupil book and cites its pages, and the guide rows below cite both the guide page and the pupil-book page the guide gives. There are no partial volumes and no duplicates in the set: every CIAM scan runs from its first chapter to its own index at the sommaire's stated page (the 6e scan opens at printed p. 3 — the cover and title leaves are not in the scan, but all teaching content is present, printed pp. 3–222 = the PDF's 220 pages exactly). The two misleading historic Drive filenames are settled by the books' own title pages: the file named “2nde SM” is the CIAM 2e Scientifique pupil book, and the file named “2nde C” is its 2e S Guide pédagogique. Link-only CIAM volumes (3e, Terminale SM/SE and their guides) are not in hand and are cited nowhere. Counting grain: one curriculum item per palier × compétence statement; one guide item per lesson the guide teaches; one pupil item per sommaire chapter. Book errors are carried corrected where they land: the Curriculum 2e année, Compétence 2, Palier 1 prints “le passionnément des objets l'espace”, a typo for “positionnement” (positioning of objects in space), and is stocked corrected; the Curriculum 3e année, Compétence 1, Palier 2 states “nombres de 1001 à 1999 (0 à 1999)” — the parenthetical contradicts the main range, and the statement is stocked by its main range; the CIAM 4e index spells PPMC as “PPCM” (trivial, noted for the record). The placements' named exclusions — the guides' remediation/application apparatus between paliers; the curricula's front matter and framework apparatus (rationale, timetables, competency-framework and assessment sections); the CIAM pupil books' end-of-chapter exercise sets, préfaces, authors' letters and closing indexes; the 2e S pupil book's Greek-alphabet page; the 2e S guide's synoptic programme tables — are stocked nowhere. Gabon COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of its pupil-textbook rows; its teacher-evidence and curriculum-requirement rows are named in the table but stay outside the count. ## Ireland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Ireland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **9 pupil textbooks of the Text & Tests series (The Celtic Press / CJ Fallon): one English volume, Text & Tests 4 (New Edition), and eight Irish-language (Téacs & Trialacha) volumes** — and placed 570 items. Ireland has no government-published textbooks: every book in the set is a commercial in-use title held as a complete free copy. The English TT4 was read in phase3-staging (byte- and SHA-256-verified in place); the eight Irish volumes were re-grabbed from their recorded COGG (cogg.ie) source URLs, each landing byte-exact at its recorded size, every file's identity proved from its cover, copyright and contents pages. The expansion map's count is right: **9 student + 0 teacher** — the one further Ireland file in hand, *Dúchas 2 — Leabhar Scileanna*, is a Junior Cycle Irish-language (Gaeilge) skills book, not Maths, and is cited nowhere in this course. Extent, honestly: five whole-course books (TT1, TT2 GL, TT3, English TT4, TT6); three strand volumes complete as published but covering one strand only (TT4 Irish = Strand 2; TT5 Irish = Strand 1; TT7 Irish = Strand 5); and one supplement booklet (the TT5 Forlíonadh — deferred Strand 1 material and the Central Limit Theorem), complete as published, not a standalone textbook. Two record flags ride with the set (the read's flagged claims F1–F2, both about the library records, not the course): **F1** — 8 of the 9 PDFs are absent from their phase3-staging folders, which hold only books.json + CURRICULUM.md; all eight are secured in GitHub and were re-grabbed byte-exact from COGG for the read, and restoring them into staging is owed on the library side. **F2** — the held TT3 is the updated edition (2024) of the 2013 book, with an added Chapter 19, Inferential Statistics, that no record mentions; its three items are stocked from that chapter and TT3's book entry names the edition wherever it appears. The older strand books and the English TT4 New Edition teach much of the same Leaving Certificate Higher Level ground in two languages and editions (e.g. TT6 chs. 1–2 ≈ TT4 Eng chs. 1–2; TT7 ch. 2 ≈ TT4 Eng ch. 7): each book's row is stocked as its own citation — they are distinct held books, not duplicates — while Ireland itself is counted once in this lesson's taught-in figure. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching section of each book. The placements' named exclusions — every book's chapter tests (“Cuir triail ort féin”), tasks, learning-experience and review sections, chapter summaries and Answers sections, and every book's prefaces, keyword lists and contents pages — are stocked nowhere. Ireland COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Ireland book is a pupil textbook. Into this lesson the placements stock 4 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (TT1), Book 2 (TT2 GL).

Morris and Deborah Crean (English ©2018), ISBN 978-1-9997795-3-5; 17,831,033 bytes, 423 PDF pp. Junior Cycle, 1st year. Printed page = PDF page − 7.

## Fiji added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Fiji's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 pupil textbooks of the Ministry of Education / Curriculum Development Unit series, one for each of Years 1–13** — and placed 1,012 items. The expansion map's count is exact: staging `books.json` holds exactly 13 Mathematics entries (one per year, all `kind: student`) and `phase3-push-state.json` lists all 13 as pushed; no Maths teacher guide is in hand (the AQEP Maths Guides for Lower Primary exist only as AnyFlip reader copies in the download list — not in hand, not counted). The staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, so each book was re-fetched for the read — from the repo tree (Contents API) where the pusher had stored it as a blob, else from the book's recorded `source_url` (a Wayback Machine capture of the ministry file, Beacon Media, or Google Drive) — and the grabbed copies were deleted after their placements were written. The read's nine flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) `textbooks.tsv` carries only 9 of the 13 rows (Years 1, 2, 3, 4, 7, 10, 11, 12, 13) — Years 5, 6, 8 and 9 are staged and pushed but missing from the TSV; (2) the Year 2 scan duplicates printed p. 57 and printed p. 58 is absent, so the Money unit's Activity 12 is not in hand to read and is cited nowhere; (3) the Year 5 capture has no cover/title leaves — the file opens at the Strand 1 divider and the body runs complete, Strand 1 p. 1 to Strand 5 p. 98, its identity resting on the staged record and the body's own structure; (4) Year 4's folio sequence is irregular — folios 44–48 and 54 never appear and folios 42 and 55 each appear twice — but the leaf count closes exactly (85 printed numbers − 6 absent + 2 duplicated = 81 body leaves), every contents strand and unit is present and no activity breaks mid-stream at the gaps, so the evidence points to the printed book's own folio misprints rather than missing leaves, a judgement the file alone cannot prove; (5) Year 3's scan order is scrambled at the end (contents folio ii and printed pp. 46, 51, 52 sit at PDF pp. 76–80) and one activity page appears twice (folioed 47 and 51 — counted once), with folio tracking accounting for all of printed pp. 1–75; (6) Year 10 exists in two packagings — the staged Beacon Media file (9,099,595 bytes, 202 pp.) is the copy cited; an ECopies capture of the same book is 9,079,205 bytes / 200 pp.; (7) Year 12's first Wayback transfer arrived truncated (11,794,148 bytes, broken cross-reference table) and was discarded — a fresh download delivered the complete book (17,085,310 bytes, 284 pp.), which is what was read; (8) Year 1's contents lists Chance at p. 65 and Data at p. 66 where the folios are 64 and 65 — the body count closes exactly, so nothing is missing and the contents is one out; (9) Year 6's interior copyright and acknowledgement pages misname it the “Year 7” book — a copy-paste error in the book itself; its cover metadata, contents, running heads and body are Year 6 throughout, and it is read and placed as Year 6. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach — among them Year 3's conversion-table lines “1,000 cm = 1 km” (correct: 100,000 cm) and “10,000 mm = 1,000 m” (correct: 10 m), Year 4's banner “30 minutes = 1 hour” against its own body's 60, Year 5's “1 000 millimetres = 1 litre”, Year 9's contents “Common Multitudes” (for Common Multiples) and its (a × b)ᵐ = aᵐ × bⁿ misprint, and Year 1's count-in-twos strip printing 9 where 19 belongs; Year 10 carries its own printed errata list (pp. 201–202), whose corrections are noted in the items. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching point — a named concept, method, rule or worked skill with its printed page. The placements' named exclusions — strand divider pages; contents and acknowledgement pages; pure exercise sets and worked-example repeats of an item already placed; Year 1's “Checking my number” ten-frame reference page (printed p. 32); Year 3's duplicated capacity page (counted once); Year 4's closing multiplication-table chart (printed p. 86); Year 10's printed errata list (pp. 201–202, apparatus — its corrections ride in the items); Year 12's closing motivational quotes and exam tips (printed pp. 273–275); Year 13's bibliography and back matter — are stocked nowhere. Fiji COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Fiji book is a pupil textbook. Into this lesson the placements stock 23 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (*Mathematics Year 3*), Book 2 (*Mathematics Year 4*), Book 3 (*Year 5 Mathematics*), Book 4 (*Year 6 Mathematics Textbook*), Book 5 (*Year 7 Mathematics Textbook*), Book 6 (*Mathematics Year 8*), Book 7 (*Form 3 Mathematics Textbook*).

Printed page = PDF page − 3 for the in-sequence body; the scan order is scrambled at the end and the capacity-discovery activity page appears twice (folioed 47 and 51), cited once.

## Honduras added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Honduras's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **21 files = 18 distinct works: 9 pupil textbooks (Grades 3–9, Grade 10 Matemática I and Grade 11 Matemática III BCH) and 12 teacher-guide files for Grades 1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 8, 9, 10 and 11, the Grade 8 guide held in two editions (2018 and the 2022 revision) and the Grade 1 and 2 guides each in two scans** — all from the Secretaría de Educación's PROMETAM / UPNFM series produced with JICA support — and placed 335 items (218 pupil-textbook items and 117 teacher-evidence items), plus 3 duplicate-edition records (placements items 241, 242 and 338) that are cited once with their works and carry no separate items. The expansion map's “9 student + 12 teacher” count is right: the staging `books.json` files, the `phase3-push-state.json` pushed set and `textbooks.tsv` agree on exactly these 21 files. There is no Grade 1–2 pupil textbook in the Secretaría's programme (workbook only — SE Manual de Lineamientos), so the Grade 1–2 teaching in hand is the two guides; there is no Grade 3 or Grade 6 teacher guide in hand. Every file was grabbed from its recorded `source_url` (educatrachos.hn / libroshonduras.com) after the staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, and verified by exact byte size against the download-list record and by page count against the `books.json` record before reading; grabbed copies were deleted after each book's text was extracted. Read basis, stated honestly: the six text-layer pupil books and eight text-layer guides were read in full from their text; the three scanned pupil books (Grades 7–9) were read by OCR of their Índices and unit/lesson openers with interior pages OCR-sampled — Grades 7 and 8 cross-checked against the full text-layer read of their own teacher guides, which plan every lesson class by class — and the two 2022 guides are themselves scans, OCR-read at cover, presentation and index level, the Grade 9 guide therefore placed at unit grain. Counting grain: one item per Índice lesson; a lesson teaching two distinct course topics is split into two items, and three items (a Grade 4 pupil lesson, a Grade 10 pupil lesson and a Grade 2 guide unit) each carry a row in two lessons below and are counted once. The placements' named exclusions — the “Páginas para recortar” cut-out pages of the Grades 3, 4 and 5 pupil books (manipulatives, not teaching text), the Grade 6 book's closing “Repaso” review, and the “Nos divertimos” / “¿Sabías que?” enrichment boxes inside placed lessons — are stocked nowhere. The read's flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) the download list §A's original 2026-10-21 mirror labels for the III-Ciclo files were swapped between Español and Matemáticas — the files it calls “Matemáticas 7/8/9 (2018 PROMETAM)” are the Español books, and its “Español 7/8/9” alternates are the Matemáticas books — a library-record fault, already corrected in `books.json`, reported here and not fixed by this merge; (2) the Grade 9 guide's cover misprints its cycle as “II Ciclo” where the programme is III Ciclo; (3) the Grade 11 pupil book's Índice misprints “Cincunferencias” for “Circunferencias”, taught corrected; (4) the 2014 scan of the Grade 1 guide has a materially poorer text layer than the mirror scan of the same work, so anyone text-mining it alone will under-read it. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach. Into this lesson the placements stock 8 items — 6 pupil-textbook rows and 2 teacher-evidence rows — from Book 2 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 4to grado*), Book 3 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 5to grado*), Book 4 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 6to grado*), Book 9 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 4to grado*), Book 10 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas and 5to grado*).

## Italy added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Italy's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **14 pupil textbooks, all adopted commercial titles (Italy has no government textbooks; schools adopt titles under the AIE/MIUR adozioni system): the Bergamini *Matematica.blu* volumes (Zanichelli) and the Sasso *Colori della Matematica* Edizione Verde and Edizione Blu volumes and triennio modules A–G (Petrini / De Agostini Scuola)** — and placed 781 items. The expansion map's “14 student + 0 teacher” count and kind split are exact against the staging `books.json` files, `textbooks.tsv` and the push state; every file was verified against its record (byte size, and MD5 where the record carries one) and read in full. Counting grain: one item per numbered theory section of the book's own Indice/Sommario. Two extent faults ride with the set, honestly: the files filed as *Matematica.blu* Vol. 1 (Book 3) and *Matematica.blu 2.0* Vol. 5 (Book 4) are each only the SECOND PART of the volume — Vol. 1 opens at Capitolo 13 (printed p. 670) and its Capitoli 1–12 and G1–G4 are not in the file; Vol. 5 opens at Capitolo 25 (printed p. 1560) and its Capitoli 18–24 are not in the file — so both are named as partial volumes wherever they are cited, only their held content is placed, and the missing first parts are not in hand anywhere and are cited nowhere (the ground the Vol. 1 first part would have covered is supplied inside this set by Books 5–6 at Sasso depth, and the Vol. 5 first part's limits and continuity ground by Book 12, Modulo G). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the eBook-only units the books' own Indici mark “(solo nell'eBook)”; and the apparatus around the numbered theory sections in every book — chapter openings, exercise blocks, laboratories, *Realtà e modelli*, exam-preparation sections (*Verso l'esame*, *Verso l'INVALSI*, *Verso l'università*), glossaries, answer keys, formula sheets, and *Approfondimento* boxes that are not numbered theory sections. Into this lesson the placements stock 6 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 5 (*Colori della Matematica — Edizione Verde, Vol. 1*) and Book 6 (*Colori della Matematica — Edizione Blu, Vol. 1*).
